W_{j}-\mathbb{E}[W_{j}\mid\hat{\bm{\eta}}]=W_{j}-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})
. Furthermore, note that:

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}

\displaystyle=\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})-P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\mathbf{W}_{*,j}

\displaystyle=P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}-P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}

(H.3)

So it is immediate that to prove the second part of the Lemma, we need to control three terms of the RHS of equation (
H.2
), whereas for the first part we need to control the last two terms of the RHS of equation (
H.2
). Therefore it is enough to control the three terms of the RHS to conclude both the parts of the Lemma. Going back to equation (
H.2
) we have using
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 4(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2})
:

\displaystyle\max_{0\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{4}{n}\max_{0\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\left\|m_{j}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})\right\|^{2}}_{T_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{4\max_{0\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}{n}}_{T_{2}}+\underbrace{4\max_{0\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\nu}_{j}}{n}}_{T_{3}}\,.

(H.4)

T_{2}
can be bounded uniformly from the functional approximation properties of B-spline basis:

T_{2}=4\max_{0\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}{n}\lesssim r_{n}^{2}\,.

For
T_{3}
, by Assumption
4.5
we have
\check{\nu}_{j}
is a centered subgaussian vector conditionally on
\hat{\eta}
with independent entries. Therefore applying Hanson-Wright inequality (
[
37
]
) we have (with
A_{n}=P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}/n
):

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\check{\nu}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\check{\nu}_{j}\mid\hat{\eta}\right]\right|>t\mid\hat{\eta}\right)\leq 2{\sf exp}\left\{\left(-c\left\{\frac{t^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{4}\left\|A_{n}\right\|_{HS}^{2}},\frac{t}{\sigma_{W}^{2}\|A_{n}\|_{op}}\right\}\right)\right\}

From the properties of the projection matrix we have:
\left\|A_{n}\right\|_{HS}^{2}=K/n^{2}
and
\|A_{n}\|_{op}=1/n
. Hence we have:

\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\check{\nu}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\nu_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\check{\nu}_{j}\mid\hat{\eta}\right]\right|>t\mid\hat{\eta}\right)\leq 2{\sf exp}\left\{\left(-c\left\{\frac{n^{2}t^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{4}K},\frac{nt}{\sigma_{W}^{2}}\right\}\right)\right\}

On the other hand, we have:

\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}}{n}\right]\leq\frac{K}{n}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j\leq p\\
|t|\leq\tau\end{subarray}}{\sf var}(W_{*,j}\mid\hat{\eta}=t):=\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\times\frac{K}{n}\,.

where by Assumption
4.5
, the variance is finite. Hence for
t>\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\times(K/n)
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\hat{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\hat{\nu}_{j}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\hat{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\hat{\nu}_{j}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}_{\hat{\eta}}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\left|\hat{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\hat{\nu}_{j}\right|>t\right|\hat{\eta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}_{\hat{\eta}}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\hat{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\hat{\nu}_{j}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\hat{\nu}_{j}^{\top}A_{n}\hat{\nu}_{j}t\right|\hat{\eta}\right]\right|>t-\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\frac{K}{n}\mid\hat{\eta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{j}\mathbb{E}_{\hat{\eta}}\left[{\sf exp}\left\{\left(-c\left\{\frac{n^{2}\left(t-\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\frac{K}{n}\right)^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{4}k},\frac{n\left(t-\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\frac{K}{n}\right)}{\sigma_{W}^{2}}\right\}\right)\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=2{\sf exp}\left\{\left(\log{p}-c\left\{\frac{n^{2}\left(t-\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\frac{K}{n}\right)^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{4}k},\frac{n\left(t-\hat{\sf var}_{\sup}\frac{K}{n}\right)}{\sigma_{W}^{2}}\right\}\right)\right\}

So this implies, with probability going to 1 we have with an appropriate choice of
t
:

\displaystyle T_{3}=4\max_{1\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}}{n}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\frac{K}{n}+\sigma_{W}^{2}\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\sqrt{\frac{K\log{p}}{n^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\dot{r}^{2}_{n}+\sigma_{W}^{2}\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\,.

(H.5)

For
T_{1}
in equation (
H.2
), we use Assumption
4.3
. Note that we have
m_{j}(\eta)=g_{j}(0,\eta)
and
\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\eta})=g_{j}(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0},\hat{\eta})
. Hence we have:

\displaystyle\frac{4}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\left\|m_{j}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=4\max_{1\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-m_{j}(\eta_{i})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=4\max_{1\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(g_{j}(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0},\hat{\eta})-g_{j}(\hat{0},\hat{\eta}_{i})+g_{j}(0,\hat{\eta})-g_{j}(0,\eta_{i})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8\max_{1\leq j\leq 1+p_{1}+p_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(g_{j}(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0},\hat{\eta}_{i})-g_{j}(0,\hat{\eta}_{i})\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(g_{j}(0,\hat{\eta}_{i})-g_{j}(0,\eta_{i})\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 8(L_{1}\vee L_{2})\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|^{2}\left(1+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\left(Z_{i}^{\top}a_{n}\right)^{2}\right)\hskip 14.45377pt\left[\text{where }a_{n}=\frac{\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}}{\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|^{2}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}\hskip 14.45377pt\left[Z\text{ is centered subgaussian with finite variance}\right]\,.

(H.6)

Combining the bounds on
T_{i}^{\prime}s
we have for the first part of the Lemma:

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}+\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

And for the second part of the Lemma:

\displaystyle\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}+\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

This completes the proof of the first and the second part of the Lemma.

For the third part:

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|}_{T_{2}}

To bound
T_{1}
we use the previous parts of this Lemma and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. which yields:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right)\right\|^{2}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j^{\prime}\leq p}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

where the bounds in the last inequality follows from the conclusions of the previous parts of this Lemma. To bound
T_{2}
we first expand
\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}
as before:

\displaystyle\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}+\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}+m_{j^{\prime}}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j^{\prime}}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})+P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}-P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j^{\prime}}

Using the above expansion we have:

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|+\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(m_{j^{\prime}}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j^{\prime}}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}\right|+\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j^{\prime}}\right|

(H.7)

The bound the first term of the RHS of the above equation, we have using Cauchy-Schwarz inequality:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}}}_{\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}1}\underbrace{\sqrt{\max_{j^{\prime}}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}}}_{\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}}\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{Equation }\eqref{eq:forgotten}]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

In the above inequality we have used the fact
\max_{j}(1/n)\left\|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}=O_{p}(1)
which follows from the following application of triangle inequality:

\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left\|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left\|\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}1\,.

as the first term is
O_{p}(1)
due to bounded variance of
\tilde{W}_{j}
(which follows from sub-gaussianity Assumption
4.5
) and the second term is
o_{p}(1)
from the previous parts of this Lemma. To bound the second term of equation (
H.1
):

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(m_{j^{\prime}}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j^{\prime}}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\left\|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}}\sqrt{\max_{j^{\prime}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\left(\check{m}_{j^{\prime}}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-m_{j^{\prime}}(\eta_{i})\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

where the last rate follows from the similar calculation as the first term of the RHS of equation (
H.4
). To bound the third term in equation (
H.1
) note that the entries of
\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}
are centered and independent conditionally on
\hat{\eta}
. Therefore we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}\right|>t\mid\hat{\bm{\eta}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\mathbb{E}\left[2{\sf exp}{\left(-c\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma_{W}\frac{\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}}{n}}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left(2\log{p}-c\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma_{W}r_{n}^{2}}\right)}

Therefore we have with probability going to 1 with an appropriate choice of
t
:

\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j^{\prime}}\right|\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

For the last term of equation (
H.1
) note that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j^{\prime}}\right|\hskip 14.45377pt\left[\text{ As }\check{\nu}_{j}=\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{j}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}\right|}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{j^{\prime}}\left|\check{\nu}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j^{\prime}}\right|}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\frac{\log{p}}{n}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

where the last inequality follows from the same calculation as for
\hat{\nu}_{j}
in the first part of this Lemma. Combining the bounds for different components of
T_{2}
we have, with probability going to
1
:

\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

This completes the proof the lemma.
∎

H.2
Proof of Lemma
A.4

To obtain
\lambda_{1}
we need to bound the
\ell_{\infty}
norm on the first term of the RHS of equation (
A.3
). We can bound that term as:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\mathbf{S}^{\perp}-\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)^{\top}\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{S}^{\perp}-\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{S}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{S}}\right)\right|}_{T_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right|}_{T_{3}}

(H.8)

Observe that, we can bound
T_{2}
via similar calculation we did to prove the third display of Lemma
A.3
as it is a special case for
j^{\prime}=1
. Therefore we have:

T_{2}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

We next bound
T_{3}
. First of all, an application
\ell_{1}
-
\ell_{\infty}
bound yields:

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]\,,

where again the last inequality follows from third conclusion of Lemma
A.3
.
To bound
T_{1}
, we further divide it into two terms:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{11}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{2\leq j\leq p}\left|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{12}}

(H.9)

To bound
T_{12}
we use the first order condition on the definition of
\theta^{*}_{S}
. Recall that
\theta^{*}_{S}
is defined as:

\theta^{*}_{S}=\mathop{\rm argmin}_{\delta}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(S-\tilde{W}_{1}^{\top}\delta\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right]

Therefore we have:

\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{W}_{-1}(S-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S})\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right]=0\,.

and consequently for any
2\leq j\leq p
and
1\leq i\leq n
, the term
\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S})\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq\tau}
is a centered subexponential and independent over
i
with
\psi_{1}
norm bounded by
\sigma^{2}_{W}\sqrt{1+\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|^{2}}
. Note that
\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|
is also bounded, which follows from the definition of
\theta^{*}_{S}
(see equation (
4.7
)):

\displaystyle\theta^{*}_{S}=\left(\Sigma_{\tau_{-1,-1}}\right)^{-1}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{W}_{-1}\tilde{S}\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}]=\left(\Sigma_{\tau_{-1,-1}}\right)^{-1}\Sigma_{\tau_{-1,1}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}\right)_{1,1}}\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}\right)_{-1,1}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}\right)_{1,1}}\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}e_{1}\right)_{-1}

This implies:

\left\|\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}=\frac{1}{\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}\right)_{1,1}^{2}}\left\|\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}e_{1}\right)_{-1}\right\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}\right)_{1,1}^{2}}\left\|\left(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1}e_{1}\right)\right\|^{2}\leq\left(\frac{\lambda_{\max}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1})}{\lambda_{\min}(\Sigma_{\tau}^{-1})}\right)^{2}\leq\left(\frac{C_{\max}}{C_{\min}}\right)^{2}

where the last bound follows from Assumption
4.6
. Hence we have:

\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S})\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq\tau}\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\leq\sigma^{2}_{W}\sqrt{1+\left(\frac{C_{\max}}{C_{\min}}\right)^{2}}

We now bound
T_{12}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|>t,\Omega_{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{j}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{i}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1,i}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}_{i}|\leq\tau}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{i}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1,i}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq\tau}\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{j}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\sum_{i}\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1,i}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}_{i}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq\tau}\right)\right|>\frac{t}{2}\right)

\displaystyle\triangleq T_{121}+T_{122}

Bounding
T_{121}
is straightforward as we have already established
\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1,i}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq t}
is sub-exponential random variable, hence applying Bernstein’s inequality we have with probability going to 1:

\displaystyle T_{121}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

(H.10)

We next bound
T_{122}
, which is trickier. Although the terms
\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1,i}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}_{i}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq\tau}\right)
are sub-exponential by similar argument as before, they are not centered. Therefore, we further need to bound the expectation of the terms, i.e.
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[W_{j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right]\right]
(as we can always restrict ourselves on
\Omega_{n}
to find the rate). Note that:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[W_{j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\left|\mathbb{E}\left[W_{j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left\|W_{j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right\|_{\alpha}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left|\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right|^{\beta}\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\left(\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau,|\eta|>\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|>\tau,|\eta|\leq\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\right]

(H.11)

We now analyze the probabilities inside the expectation. Fix
\rho>0
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau,|\eta|>\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau,|\eta|>(1+\rho)\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau,\tau<|\eta|\leq(1+\rho)\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\hat{\eta}-\eta\right|>\rho\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\tau<|\eta|\leq(1+\rho)\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|Z^{\top}a_{n}\right|>\frac{\rho\tau}{\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|}\right)+\|f_{\eta}\|_{\infty}\rho\tau

\displaystyle\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left(-\frac{c\rho^{2}\tau^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{2}\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)}+\|f_{\eta}\|_{\infty}\rho\tau

(H.12)

Similar calculation also yields:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|>\tau,|\eta|\leq\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left(-\frac{c\rho^{2}\tau^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{2}\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)}+\|f_{\eta}\|_{\infty}\rho\tau\,.

(H.13)

Therefore we have from equation (
H.11
):

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[W_{j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\left(4{\sf exp}{\left(-\frac{c\rho^{2}\tau^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{2}\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)}+2\|f_{\eta}\|_{\infty}\rho\tau\right)^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\right]

\displaystyle=\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\left[4{\sf exp}{\left(-\frac{c\rho^{2}\tau^{2}}{\sigma_{W}^{2}\|\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma_{0}\|_{2}^{2}}\right)}+2\|f_{\eta}\|_{\infty}\rho\tau\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\left(\left[4{\sf exp}{\left(-\frac{c\rho^{2}\tau^{2}}{C\sigma_{W}^{2}\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right)}+2\|f_{\eta}\|_{\infty}\rho\tau\right]\right)^{\frac{1}{\beta}}

Choosing
\rho=(\sqrt{C}\sigma_{W}/\tau\sqrt{c})\sqrt{((s_{\gamma}\log{p})/n)\log{(n/(s_{\gamma}\log{p}))}}
we have:

\displaystyle\alpha\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\left(\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau,|\eta|>\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(|\hat{\eta}|>\tau,|\eta|\leq\tau\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha\left(\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}\log{\left(\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}\right)}\right)^{\frac{1}{2\beta}}

\displaystyle\triangleq\alpha t_{n}^{\frac{1}{\beta}}\hskip 14.45377pt\left[t_{n}=\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}\log{\left(\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}\right)}}\right]\,.

The above equation is true for all
\alpha,\beta>1
such that
1/\alpha+1/\beta=1
. Therefore minimizing the expression over
\alpha,\beta
we have that the optimal choice of
\alpha
is
\alpha=\log{(1/t_{n})}
which yields that:

\displaystyle\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathds{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\mathbb{E}\left[W_{j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{1}\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\log{\frac{1}{t_{n}}}t_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{\log{\frac{1}{t_{n}}}}}

\displaystyle=t_{n}\log{\frac{1}{t_{n}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}\,.

Using this bounds on the expectation of
\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1,i}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\left(\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}_{i}|\leq\tau}-\mathds{1}_{|\eta_{i}|\leq\tau}\right)
we have:

T_{122}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}=\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}\,.

Combining the bound on
T_{121}
and
T_{122}
we have:

T_{12}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}\,.

To bound
T_{11}
not that from equation (
H.2
) we have:

\displaystyle\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}

\displaystyle=\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}+\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}

\displaystyle=\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}+\left[m_{j}(\eta)-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\eta})\right]+P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}-P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}

Using this we have:

\displaystyle T_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{111}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(m_{j}(\eta)-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\eta})\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{112}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{113}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{114}}

We start with bounding
T_{111}
. As mentioned previously, we have
\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}
is active for
p_{1}+2\leq j\leq p_{1}+p_{2}+1
and for such a
j
:

\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}^{\perp}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{i,j}=Z_{i,j}(\hat{b}^{\prime}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-b(\eta_{i}))\,.

Using this, we have:

\displaystyle T_{111}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{p_{1}+2\leq j\leq p_{1}+p_{2}+1}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\mathbf{W}^{\perp}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\sqrt{\max_{p_{1}+2\leq j\leq p_{1}+p_{2}+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(\hat{b}^{\prime}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-b^{\prime}(\eta_{i})\right)^{2}}}_{T_{1111}}\times\underbrace{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}}}_{T_{1112}}

It follows from WLLN that
T_{1112}=O_{p}(1)
. For
T_{1111}
similar calculation as of (
H.1
) yields:

\displaystyle T_{1111}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\max_{p_{1}+2\leq j\leq p_{1}+p_{2}+1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(\hat{b}^{\prime}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-b(\eta_{i})\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(\hat{b}^{\prime}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-b^{\prime}(\hat{\eta}_{i})\right)^{2}}+\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(b^{\prime}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-b^{\prime}(\eta_{i})\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}\times\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}}+\|b^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(\hat{\eta}_{i}-\eta_{i}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}\times\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}}+\|b^{\prime}\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\sqrt{\max_{j}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(Z_{i}^{\top}a_{n}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

Note that in the above analysis we have the used the facts:

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq p_{2}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{Lemma }\ref{lem:subg_second_moment}]

\displaystyle\max_{1\leq j\leq p_{2}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}Z_{i,j}^{2}\left(Z_{i}^{\top}a_{n}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{p}(1)\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{Lemma }\ref{lem:subg_fourth_moment}]\,.

which follows from the subgaussianity of
Z
. Therefore the above bounds yield:

T_{111}\leq T_{1111}\times T_{1112}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

To bound
T_{112}
, we use Cauchy-Schwarz inequality to show that with probability going to 1:

\displaystyle T_{112}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(m_{j}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|m_{j}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{j}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})\right\|^{2}}\times\underbrace{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}}}_{O_{p}(1)}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p_{2}}}{n}}\,.

For
T_{113}
, we further sub-divide it as follows:

\displaystyle T_{113}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{1131}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}\right)\right|}_{T_{1132}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right|}_{T_{1133}}

(H.14)

To bound
T_{1131}
, note that
\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P_{\hat{\mathbf{N}}_{k}}^{\perp}
is measurable with respect to
\hat{\eta}
, whereas
\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}
has mean zero conditional on
\hat{\eta}
. Therefore, from subgaussian concentration inequality we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P_{\hat{\mathbf{N}}_{k}}^{\perp}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P_{\hat{\mathbf{N}}_{k}}^{\perp}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{E}_{\hat{\eta}}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P_{\hat{\mathbf{N}}_{k}}^{\perp}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|>t\mid\hat{\eta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{E}_{\hat{\eta}}\left[2{\sf exp}{\left(-c\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma_{W}\frac{\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}{n}}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left(\log{p}-c\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma_{W}r_{n}^{2}}\right)}

Therefore we have with probability going to
1
:

T_{1131}=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P_{\hat{\mathbf{N}}_{k}}^{\perp}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

For
T_{1132}
of equation (
H.14
) we apply CS inequality and use some already derived bounds to conclude that with probability going to 1:

\displaystyle T_{1132}=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

For
T_{1133}
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}P^{\perp}_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j^{\prime}\leq p}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

which concludes:

T_{1133}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

Combining the bounds on the different parts of
T_{113}
we have:

T_{113}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \left(1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\right)r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

Finally, to bound
T_{114}
, we first expand it as we just did for the residual term
\check{\mathbf{R}}_{j}
:

\displaystyle T_{114}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

(H.15)

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|}_{T_{1141}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}\right)\right|}_{T_{1142}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right|}_{T_{1143}}

(H.16)

To bound
T_{1141}
, we use the same technique we use to bound
(\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j})/n
in the proof of Lemma
A.3
. Recall that we use Hanson-Wright inequality using the fact that
\check{\nu}_{j}
has mean 0 conditional on
\hat{\eta}
and
P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}
is measurable with respect to
\hat{\eta}
. Therefore we have:

\displaystyle T_{1141}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \left(\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)^{2}\,.

For
T_{1142}
of equation (
H.16
) we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{S}}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \left(\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

Similarly for
T_{1143}
of equation (
H.16
) we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j^{\prime}\leq p}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j^{\prime}}-\hat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j^{\prime}}\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\sqrt{\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)}\times\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p_{2}}}{n}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\left(\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p_{2}}}{n}}\,.

Aggregating the bound on the different parts of
T_{114}
we have:

T_{114}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \left(\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\left(\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}+(1+\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1})\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right)\,.

This completes the bounds for
T_{11}
, which yields:

T_{11}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}+\left(1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\right)\left(\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\vee\sqrt{s_{\gamma}}\frac{\log{p}}{n}\right)\,.

Combining the bounds for
T_{11}
and
T_{12}
we have:

T_{1}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+\left(1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\right)\left(r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\vee\sqrt{s_{\gamma}}\frac{\log{p}}{n}\right)\,.

So far we have:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+\left(1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1}\right)\left(\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\vee\sqrt{s_{\gamma}}\frac{\log{p}}{n}\right)\,,

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]\,,

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ (1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1})\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]\,.

which implies:

\displaystyle T_{1}+T_{2}+T_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ (1+\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1})\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\vee\sqrt{s_{\gamma}}\frac{\log{p}}{n}\right]\asymp\lambda_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}(1+\|\theta^{*}_{S}\|_{1})\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\asymp\lambda_{1}\,.

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{s_{\gamma}\log{p}/n}\ll\dot{r}_{n}
and
\sqrt{s_{\gamma}}(\log{p}/n)\ll\sqrt{(s_{\gamma}\log{p})/n}
.

We next find the appropriate bound on
\lambda_{0}
. From equation (
A.3
), the value of
\lambda_{0}
depends on the the bound on the
\ell_{\infty}
norm of the first term of RHS. We start with the similar division as we used for
\lambda_{1}
as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}\right)^{\top}\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}\right)\right|}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\right|}_{T_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\underbrace{\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\theta^{*}_{Y}\right|}_{T_{3}}

Observe that,
T_{3}
here is identical to the term
T_{3}
in equation (
H.8
) in estimating
\lambda_{1}
only
\theta^{*}_{S}
replaced by
\theta^{*}_{Y}
. Therefore, from a direct approach to our previous calculations we conclude that with probability going to 1:

T_{3}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \|\theta^{*}_{Y}\|_{1}\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]\,.

T_{2}
can also be bounded along the same line of argument used to prove the third display of Lemma
A.3
.
An expansion of
\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}
along the line of equation (
H.2
) yields:

\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}=m_{0}(\bm{\eta})-\check{m}_{0}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})+P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}-P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{0}\,.

where
\check{\nu}_{0}=\mathbf{Y}-\check{m}_{0}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})
is subgaussian with constant
\sigma_{Y}
. Thereby following the line of argument as of Lemma
A.3
we have:

\frac{1}{n}\left\|\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right\|^{2}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}+\left[\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right]

On the other hand for the cross term we expand it as:

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\right|}_{T_{21}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\right|}_{T_{22}}

For
T_{21}
, by CS inequality we have:

\displaystyle T_{21}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right)\right\|^{2}}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\right\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\hskip 14.45377pt[\text{Lemma }\ref{lem:sq_rate}]\,.

To bound
T_{22}
:

\displaystyle T_{22}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(m_{0}(\eta)-\check{m}_{0}(\hat{\eta})\right)\right|}_{T_{221}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right|}_{T_{222}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{0}\right|}_{T_{223}}

(H.17)

To bound
T_{221}
of equation (
H.17
), first note that:

\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|\left(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right)\right\|^{2}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}1\,.

where the last inequality follows from the fact that
(1/n)\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\|^{2}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}1
(as
{\sf var}(W_{-j})
has uniformly bounded variances over
j
and
W_{j}
’s are uniformly sub-gaussian) and as we have established in Lemma
A.3

(1/n)\max_{j}\|(\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}-\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j})\|^{2}=o_{p}(1)
.
Therefore we have:

\displaystyle T_{221}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(m_{0}(\eta)-\check{m}_{0}(\hat{\eta})\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left\|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}}}_{\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}1}\times\underbrace{\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\left(\check{m}_{0}(\hat{\eta}_{i})-m_{0}(\eta_{i})\right)^{2}}}_{\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

where the bound
(1/n)\|\check{m}_{0}(\hat{\bm{\eta}})-m_{0}(\bm{\eta})\|^{2}
follows from equation (
H.6
).

To bound
T_{222}
of equation (
H.17
), note that the entries of
\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}
are centered and independent conditionally on
\hat{\eta}
. Therefore we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right|>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right|>t\mid\hat{\eta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{1\leq j\leq p}\mathbb{E}\left[2{\sf exp}{\left(-c\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma_{W}\frac{\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}^{\top}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}}{n}}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left(2\log{p}-c\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma_{W}r_{n}^{2}}\right)}

Using an appropriate choice of
t
we conclude that with probability going to 1:

T_{222}=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}^{\perp}\check{\mathbf{R}}_{0}\right|\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ r_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\,.

For the last term of equation (
H.1
) note that:

T_{223}=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{0}\right|=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{0}\right|

as by definition
\check{\nu}_{j}=\widehat{\tilde{\mathbf{W}}}_{*,j}=\mathbf{W}_{*,j}-\mathbb{E}[W_{j}\mid\hat{\bm{\eta}}]
. Therefore an application of Cauchy-Schwarz inequality yields:

T_{223}\leq\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\check{\nu}_{j}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{j}\right|}\times\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\left|\check{\nu}_{0}^{\top}P_{\mathbf{N}_{k}}\check{\nu}_{0}\right|}

Now we can bound both the terms of the above equation via similar calculation used to yield the rate in equation (
H.5
). Therefore we have:

T_{223}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\,.

Combining the bounds for different components of
T_{22}
we have, with probability going to
1
:

T_{22}=\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}\right)\right|\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right]+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

This implies we can bound
T_{2}
as:

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\ \left[\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right]+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

Combining the bounds on
T_{2}
and
T_{3}
we have:

T_{2}+T_{3}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}(1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{Y}\|_{1})\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]\,.

Finally for
T_{1}
we expand it as before:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right)^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}\right)\right|}_{T_{11}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\left|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\top}\left(\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}\right)\right|}_{T_{12}}

To bound
T_{12}
we have to adopt similar approach taken to bound
T_{12}
of the analysis of
\lambda_{1}
(equation (
H.9
)). The reason is also same, i.e. the term
\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,j}(\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}_{i}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{i,-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{Y})\mathds{1}(|\hat{\eta}_{i}|\leq\tau)
is not centered and consequently to apply Bernstein’s inequality we need to bound the expectation of the above term. For brevity, we won’t repeat the same calculation here as the same calculation will lead us to same conclusion:

T_{12}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}\,.

Analysis of
T_{11}
is also similar to the
T_{11}
term in the derivation of
\lambda_{1}
, with
\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}
is now replaced by
\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}
. Further, as mentioned previously, we need to the careful about the subgaussian constant
\sigma_{Y}
of
\tilde{\mathbf{Y}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}
. This yields that with probability going to
1
:

T_{11}\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\left(r_{n}^{2}+\frac{\log{p}}{n}\right)+\left\{1+(1+\|\theta^{*}_{Y}\|_{1})\left(r_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right\}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}

Hence we have:

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=T_{11}+T_{12}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}\mathbb{P}\left(r_{n}^{2}+\frac{\log{p}}{n}\right)+\left\{\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+(1+\|\theta^{*}_{Y}\|_{1})\left(r_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right\}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}r_{n}^{2}+\left\{\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}+(1+\|\theta^{*}_{Y}\|_{1})\left(r_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right\}\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\,.

Finally, combining the bounds on
T_{1},T_{2},T_{3}
we conclude:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\mathbf{Y}^{\perp}-\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{Y}\right)^{\top}\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\right\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}(1\vee\|\theta^{*}_{Y}\|_{1})\left[\dot{r}_{n}+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\right]+\sqrt{\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}}\left(\log{\frac{n}{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}}\right)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\asymp\lambda_{0}\,.

This completes the proof of this lemma.

H.3
Proof of Proposition
A.6

A basic expansion yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\mathbf{S}^{\perp}-\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\hat{\theta}_{-1,1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\mathbf{S}^{\perp}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\left(\hat{\theta}_{-1,1}-\theta^{*}_{S}\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{n^{\prime}_{3}}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}

(H.18)

That the first term on the RHS of equation (
H.18
) is
O_{p}(1)
directly follows from assumption
4.6
. The bound on the second term was already established is the proof of Lemma
A.3
which ensures that this term is
o_{p}(1)
. The asymptotic negligibility of the third term directly follows from the prediction consistency of LASSO. Finally for the last term, define
\mathcal{S}_{1}
to be the set of active elements in
\theta^{*}_{S}
. By our assumption, we have
|\mathcal{S}_{1}|\leq s_{1}
. Hence we have:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}\right)\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda_{\max}\left(\frac{\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}\right)}{n^{\prime}_{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{3}}{\sf tr}\left(\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}\right)^{\top}\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n_{3}}\sum_{j\in\mathcal{S}_{1}}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{*,j}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbb{P}}s_{1}\left\{\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}+\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right\}\,.

where the last line follows from the second part of Lemma
A.3
. Now we have presented some sufficient condition in subsection
A.4
of the main document (especially (
A.16
), (
A.17
)) under which the above bound in
o(1)
. Therefore under those sufficient condition, we have established that:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\mathbf{S}^{\perp}-\breve{\mathbf{W}}^{\perp}_{-1}\hat{\theta}_{-1,1}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{3}}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{S}}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\theta^{*}_{S}\right\|^{2}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{n/3}\left(\tilde{S}_{i}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{|\hat{\eta}_{i}|\leq\tau}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n_{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{n/3}\left(\tilde{S}_{i}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}+o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tilde{S}-\tilde{W}_{-1}^{\top}\theta^{*}_{S}\right)^{2}\mathds{1}_{|\eta|\leq\tau}\right]

This completes the proof.

H.4
Proof of Proposition
A.5

As per assumption
4.7
, we have some constants
\kappa,c>0
such that with high probability:

\inf_{\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\leq c\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}}\frac{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\Delta\right\|^{2}}{\left\|\Delta\right\|^{2}}\geq\kappa\,.

As we regress both
\mathbf{Y}
and
\mathbf{S}
on
\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}
, the set
S
here generically used to denote the active set of both
\theta^{*}_{S}
and
\theta^{*}_{Y}
. We will show that the above inequality also holds for
\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}
some
\kappa^{\prime}
(which can be taken as
\kappa/2
for all large
n
) with high probabiltiy. Towards that end, first triangle inequality yields:

\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}\Delta\right\|\geq\left\|\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\Delta\right\|-\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\Delta\right\|

We next show that with probability going to 1,

\sup_{\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\leq c\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}}\frac{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\Delta\right\|^{2}}{\|\Delta\|^{2}}=o_{p}(1)

which will complete the proof. Note that we have:

\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\Delta\right\|^{2}=\sum_{j=1}^{p}\Delta^{2}_{j}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}+\sum_{j\neq k}\Delta_{j}\Delta_{k}\left\langle\mathbf{W}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j},\mathbf{W}_{*,k}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,k}\right\rangle

which yields the following bound:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\Delta\right\|^{2}}{\|\Delta\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\max_{j}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}\left(1+\sup_{\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\leq c\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}}\frac{\|\Delta\|_{1}^{2}}{\|\Delta\|^{2}}\right)

For any
\Delta
with
\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\leq c\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}
we have:

\displaystyle\|\Delta\|_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}+\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(c+1)^{2}\|\Delta_{S}\|^{2}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq(c+1)^{2}\|\Delta_{S}\|^{2}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq s(c+1)^{2}\|\Delta_{S}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq s(c+1)^{2}\|\Delta\|^{2}

Hence, we have:

\sup_{\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\leq c\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}}\frac{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\Delta\right\|^{2}}{\|\Delta\|^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{n}\max_{j}\left\|\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{*,j}\right\|^{2}(1\vee s)

where
s=s_{0}\vee s_{1}
. Using Lemma
A.3
we conclude with probability approaching to 1:

\sup_{\|\Delta_{S^{c}}\|_{1}\leq c\|\Delta_{S}\|_{1}}\frac{\frac{1}{n}\left\|\left(\breve{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}^{\perp}-\tilde{\mathbf{W}}_{-1}\right)\Delta\right\|^{2}}{\|\Delta\|^{2}}\lesssim(s_{0}\vee s_{1})\left(\left[\dot{r}_{n}^{2}+\left(\frac{\log{p}}{n}\vee\dot{r}_{n}\sqrt{\frac{\log{p}}{n}}\right)\right]\vee\frac{s_{\gamma}\log{p}}{n}\right)

Again, as in the case of for the proof of Proposition
A.6
, the above bound is
o(1)
under certain sufficient condition as discussed in detail in subsection
A.4
of the main document. Under those sufficient conditions, we establish the RE condition.

Appendix I
Supplementary lemmas

Lemma I.1
.

Suppose
X,Y
are two sub-exponential random variables, i.e.
\|X\|_{\psi_{1}}
and
\|Y\|_{\psi_{1}}
are finite. Then we have:

\|XY\|_{\psi_{1/2}}\leq\|X\|_{\psi_{1}}\|Y\|_{\psi_{1}}\,.

Proof.

The proof follows along the similar line of arguments used in the proof of Lemma 2.7.7 of
[
46
]
, which we present here for the sake of completeness. Without loss of generality assume that
\|X\|_{\psi_{1}}=\|Y\|_{\psi_{1}}=1
(otherwise we can always scale by it).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[e^{\sqrt{|XY|}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[e^{\frac{|X|+|Y|}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[e^{\frac{|X|}{2}}e^{\frac{|Y|}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\left[e^{|X|}+e^{|Y|}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2\,.

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma I.2
.

Suppose
X
be a
n\times p
matrix with i.i.d. rows and suppose each co-ordinates are centered sub-gaussian with sub-gaussian constant
\sigma
. Then we have:

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i,j}^{2}>3\sigma^{2}\right)\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left[\log{p}-cn\right]}

for some constant
c
.

Proof.

From the sub-gaussianity of
X_{i,j}
we have
\mathbb{E}[X_{i,j}^{2}]\leq 2\sigma^{2}
. Furthermore we know
X_{i,j}^{2}
is sub-exponential with
\|X_{i,j}^{2}\|_{\psi_{1}}=\|X_{i,j}\|^{2}_{\psi_{2}}=\sigma^{2}
. Therefore using Bernstein inequality we have for any
1\leq j\leq p
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i,j}^{2}-2\sigma^{2}>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i,j}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{2}]>t+(2\sigma^{2}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{2}])\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}X_{i,j}^{2}-\mathbb{E}[X_{j}^{2}]>t\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left[-c\min\left(\frac{n^{2}t^{2}}{n\sigma^{4}},\frac{nt}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\right]}

\displaystyle=2{\sf exp}{\left[-c\min\left(\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma^{4}},\frac{nt}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\right]}

Therefore, an application of union bound yields:

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}X_{i,j}^{2}-2\sigma^{2}>t\right)\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left[\log{p}-c\min\left(\frac{nt^{2}}{\sigma^{4}},\frac{nt}{\sigma^{2}}\right)\right]}

If we take
t=\sigma^{2}
, we have:

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}X_{i,j}^{2}>3\sigma^{2}\right)\leq 2{\sf exp}{\left[\log{p}-cn\right]}=o(1)

as long as
\log{p}/n\to 0
.
∎

Lemma I.3
.

Suppose
X_{1},\dots,X_{n}
are i.i.d centered sub-gaussian random vector (with sub-gaussian constant
\sigma
) in dimension
p
. Then for any two vectors
a,b\in S^{p-1}
we have:

\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}>(2+C_{1})\mu\right)\leq 2e^{\log{p}-\frac{\sqrt{n\mu}}{\log{(n+1)}}}

for all large
n
, for some constant
C_{1}
(involves
\sigma
) and
\mu
as the mean of
\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}
. Moreover,
\mu
is bounded by
\sigma^{4}
and consequently we have:

max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}=O_{p}(1)

as long as
(\log{p}\log{n})/\sqrt{n}\to 0
.

Proof.

As
X_{i}
’s are sub-gaussian, we have:

\mu\triangleq\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}\right]\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{4}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{4}\right]}\leq 16\sigma^{2}\,.

From Lemma
I.1
, it is immediate that:

\left\|\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}\right\|_{\psi_{1/2}}\leq\left\|\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\right\|_{\psi_{1}}\left\|\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\right\|_{\psi_{1}}=\sigma^{4}\,.

For the rest of the proof, we use Theorem 3.2 of
[
22
]
with
\alpha=1/2
, which yields:

\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}-\mu\right|_{\psi_{1/2},L_{n}(1/2)}\leq\frac{2e\sqrt{6}\sigma^{4}}{\sqrt{n}}

with

L_{n}(1/2)\leq C\sigma^{8}\frac{\log^{2}{(n+1)}}{\sqrt{n}}\,.

Therefore using the tail bound of
[
22
]
(last display of page 8) we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}-\mu>\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{n}}\left\{\sqrt{t}+\frac{t^{2}\log^{2}{(n+1)}}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}\right)\leq 2e^{-t}\,.

Choosing
t=\sqrt{n\mu}/\log{(n+1)}
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}>(2+C_{1})\mu\right)\leq 2e^{-\frac{\sqrt{n\mu}}{\log{(n+1)}}}\,.

Therefore, a simple application of union bound yields:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{1\leq j\leq p}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(X_{i}^{\top}a\right)^{2}\left(X_{i}^{\top}b\right)^{2}>(2+C_{1})\mu\right)\leq 2e^{\log{p}-\frac{\sqrt{n\mu}}{\log{(n+1)}}}\,.

where the right hand side of the above equation in
o(1)
as long as
(\log{p}\log{n})/\sqrt{n}\to 0
.
∎
